this is referenced from both locale/en-us/Text.package - locale~/space_diplomacy~.locale, and Spore_Game.package\space_conversations~\space_diplomacy~.cnv, as well as other fles from this folder
the line breaks in the original locales were just "~p~"
ive retained some values and actions that i cant make sense of, but might be useful
i also kept the dialogue order the same for both the sets of dialogues and also the lines within them. if the order seems weird thats on the original space_diplomacy~.cnv file
however all the eventlog strings from the mission localization files were excluded
